Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in molecules theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 839 and the invariant I(A,B) = 147 mod 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the molecules space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 839 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 147. QED.
